# Spec revision 66: what changed in the records

Generated by `tools/record_changes.py`; do not edit. Each generated record as committed at
`0359119` (2026-11-20: the records of revision 64's regeneration; revision 65 had none), against the
record as read at commit `41b14c3`, the last before revision 67's regeneration moved the records, after revision 66's one regeneration (T-258); both sides are read from git, so this page is frozen. Revision 66 is revision 65's engine batch
with extra reactive acts under anxiety (`M4.D.3c`), CONFLICT consuming capacity (`M4.D.3d`) and scripted divorce
(`M7.C.1m`); the engine hash moved, so every record was rerun. A changed verdict is a new result under new rules,
reported beside revision 64's and version 1's verdicts and never in their place (`M11.F`); a premise passing is
never a finding (`M11.5`). It is not a
defect. A row's verdict is its outcome (a criterion, or a criterion under a sweep setting) or whether the
criterion caught the mutant (a mutation row). An effect **moved** when its mean difference changed by more than
the half-width revision 64's record stated; moved effects are listed only for rows whose verdict held. "Differ at
all" counts every field of a row on both sides that is not exactly revision 64's (the verdict, the seed count, the
flags, each readout's mean difference, half-width and p), including a field on one side only; each is listed
under its record, and "No change" means none differs. A record's hash line must differ from revision 64's, or the
tool refuses: an unchanged hash means it was not regenerated. A record with no row on both sides is refused too.
